The Blue Ridge Butchery facility, the mobile processing units, and the value-added processing capabilities represent a growing food processing sector that supports the region's agricultural heritage while embracing innovation and sustainability. The future of food processing in the region is bright, and its growth is central to the broader economic revitalization of the Tazewell County region.

# Chapter Nine: Pillar Three - Manufacturing and Machine Making

## Section 9.1: Heavy Metal Fabrication and Mining Equipment

### The Strategic Importance of Advanced Manufacturing

The manufacturing sector in Tazewell County represents a vital pillar of the region's diversified economy, building on the industrial heritage established during the coal era while embracing new technologies, new markets, and new opportunities for growth. The heavy metal fabrication and mining equipment manufacturers that call the region home have demonstrated remarkable resilience and adaptability, transitioning from a dependence on the coal industry to a diversified customer base that spans multiple sectors. This adaptability is essential to the region's economic future, as the decline of coal has necessitated the development of new markets and new products that can sustain manufacturing employment in the region.

The strategic importance of manufacturing to Tazewell County's economy cannot be overstated. The sector provides stable, well-paying jobs that support families and communities, generating economic activity that ripples through the broader economy. The manufacturing sector also provides a market for the region's workforce, utilizing the skills that have been developed over generations of industrial employment. The presence of a strong manufacturing sector makes the region more attractive to other businesses, creating a virtuous cycle of investment and growth that supports the broader economic development of the region.

The manufacturing sector in Tazewell County is characterized by its diversity, with companies serving a range of industries including mining, energy, automotive, material handling, and aerospace. This diversity provides a buffer against downturns in any single industry, as companies can shift their production to serve other markets when demand in one sector declines. The diversity of the manufacturing sector is a strength that has enabled the region to weather the decline of coal and to build a more resilient industrial base for the future.

The support of the Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority (VCEDA) and other economic development organizations has been essential to the growth of the manufacturing sector. VCEDA has provided loans and grants to manufacturing companies in the region, supporting expansion and modernization projects that have created jobs and increased competitiveness. The partnership between the public and private sectors has been essential to the success of the manufacturing sector, with the public sector providing the financing and infrastructure that enable private companies to invest and grow.

### Lawrence Brothers, Inc.: From a Three-Car Garage to a Global Leader

Lawrence Brothers, Inc. (LBI) represents one of the most remarkable success stories in Tazewell County's manufacturing sector, demonstrating how a small family business can grow into a global leader through strategic diversification and continuous investment. The company was founded in 1974 by brothers Jim and Dale Lawrence, who started a welding business in a three-car garage in Springville, Virginia, where they fabricated mine battery trays for Exide Battery, now known as EnerSys . This humble beginning, with its roots in the coal industry that had defined the region for generations, would evolve into a major manufacturing enterprise serving customers around the world.

The company's growth was steady and strategic, with the addition of Gould Battery as a customer prompting the acquisition of a site just outside of Bluefield in 1976, where a 10,500-square-foot building was constructed . This initial facility, modest by today's standards, provided the space needed for the company to expand its operations and to serve its growing customer base. The location in Bluefield, near the heart of the coalfields and with access to transportation infrastructure, provided a strategic advantage that would serve the company well in the decades to come.

Today, Lawrence Brothers has grown to more than 80,000 square feet of building space and employs more than 100 people, making it one of the largest manufacturers in Tazewell County . The company's growth has been driven by a strategy of continuous investment in facilities, equipment, and workforce training, with ownership committed to maintaining the company's position at the forefront of the battery tray industry. The company's facility expansion from 10,500 to more than 80,000 square feet reflects the growth of the business and the diversification of its product lines.

The evolution of ownership at Lawrence Brothers reflects the family's commitment to the business and to the region. Mark Lawrence, son of founder Jim Lawrence, had been with the company since his high school days, serving in nearly every capacity on the shop floor . Today, the company is owned and operated by Mark's daughter Melanie Protti-Lawrence and her husband Fernando Protti, who joined the company in 2006 and now each own 50 percent of the company . The continuity of family ownership has provided stability and a long-term perspective that has enabled the company to invest in diversification and growth.

### The Market Dominance of Lawrence Brothers, Inc.

Lawrence Brothers has achieved a remarkable position in its core markets, with the company holding 45 percent of the market share of mine battery trays, which are utilized in battery-powered equipment produced by major manufacturers including Komatsu, Caterpillar, and General Electric . This dominant market position reflects the company's reputation for quality, reliability, and innovation in the design and fabrication of battery trays for the mining industry. The company's products are used in equipment that operates in some of the most demanding environments in the world, requiring the highest standards of quality and durability.

The company's market share extends beyond mining to include material handling applications, where Lawrence Brothers holds a 50 percent market share of all material handling battery boxes in the motive power, ground support, and cold warehousing industries . This diversification into material handling has been a key element of the company's strategy, reducing its dependence on the mining industry and providing access to growing markets. The material handling battery boxes are used in a range of applications, including forklifts, ground support equipment, and cold storage facilities.

The company's customer list includes many major battery manufacturers, such as EnerSys, Deka, and Crown, with the battery systems found worldwide in equipment manufactured by JOY/Komatsu, General Electric, and others . The company's products are utilized in a range of applications, from mining operations to motive power and material handling, demonstrating the versatility of the company's fabrication capabilities. The company's ability to serve diverse customers and applications reflects its expertise in heavy metal fabrication and its commitment to quality.

The significance of Lawrence Brothers' market position extends beyond the direct economic impact to encompass the company's role in the regional economy. As one of the largest employers in Tazewell County, the company provides stable employment for more than 100 families, generating economic activity that ripples through the broader economy. The company's success also provides a model for other manufacturers in the region, demonstrating that it is possible to build a global business from a modest beginning in the Appalachian coalfields.

### The Fiber Optic Laser Expansion: A 75% Capacity Increase

The most significant recent investment at Lawrence Brothers has been the acquisition of a Mitsubishi fiber optic laser, which has increased the company's capacity by approximately 75 percent and enabled it to embrace more technical projects with both existing and new customers . The fiber optic laser, a fully automated 10,000-watt system, represents a significant upgrade from the company's previous equipment, providing faster cutting speeds, greater precision, and the ability to cut a wider range of materials. The acquisition of the laser was supported by a $1.315 million loan from VCEDA, which closed in April 2025 .

The investment in the fiber optic laser was described by Melanie Protti-Lawrence, president of Lawrence Brothers, as a strategic acquisition that will "not only boost our capacity, but also enable us to embrace more technical projects both with existing and new customers" . The laser's capabilities are enabling the company to expand its product offerings and to serve customers in industries that require more complex fabrication, further diversifying the company's customer base. The laser technology also improves the efficiency of the company's operations, reducing production time and costs.

The fiber optic laser expansion follows a previous investment in 2019, when VCEDA approved a $600,000 loan to help the company purchase a new laser and associated costs . That investment was highly successful, with the company adding 25-30 new jobs ahead of schedule – within less than two years of the loan closing . The success of the 2019 investment provided the confidence for the company to make the larger investment in 2025, demonstrating the effectiveness of the partnership between the company and VCEDA. The company's ability to create jobs ahead of schedule reflects the strength of its business model and the demand for its products.

The impact of the fiber optic laser expansion on the company's workforce has been significant. The new equipment requires skilled operators, creating opportunities for workforce development and training. The company has developed an on-site paid apprenticeship program to upskill both incoming workers and existing employees in higher-skilled and more competitively compensated career paths such as automation programming, maintenance, and operation . This investment in workforce development is essential to the company's long-term success, ensuring that it has the skilled labor needed to operate its advanced equipment.

### Diversification Strategy: Beyond Mining to New Markets

The diversification strategy that has driven Lawrence Brothers' growth has its roots in the recognition that dependence on the coal industry was a vulnerability that threatened the company's long-term viability. Since 2006, when Melanie Protti-Lawrence and Fernando Protti joined the company, there has been an increased focus on diversification, expanding the company's business services into fields outside the mine industry . This strategic pivot has been essential to the company's survival and growth, reducing its exposure to the boom-and-bust cycles of the coal industry.

Currently, about 50 percent of the company's customer base is mining-related, reflecting the success of the diversification strategy . While the company still manufactures, services, and rebuilds mine battery trays, it has also developed a range of products for other industries, including transformer components, furniture platforms, forklift trays, and decorative metal art. The company's primary products are steel enclosures for motive power and material handling industries, with areas of diversification including transformer components, battery and energy storage systems, material handling equipment, small industrial parts manufacturing, ground support and airline industry, and custom metal fabrication .

The diversification of Lawrence Brothers has been supported by continuous improvement in its facility, equipment, and employee training, with the company remaining at the forefront of battery tray fabrication and other related steel manufacturing . The company's ability to serve diverse customers and applications reflects its expertise in heavy metal fabrication, its commitment to quality, and its willingness to invest in new capabilities. The company's products may be found throughout the world, reflecting the global reach of its customer base and the quality of its fabrication.

The economic impact of Lawrence Brothers' diversification extends beyond the company itself to the broader region. The company's success provides a model for other manufacturers, demonstrating that it is possible to build a diversified business in the region. The company's investment in equipment and workforce development creates jobs and economic activity that benefits the broader community. The company's diversification also provides a stable source of employment that is less vulnerable to the fluctuations of the coal industry.

### Workforce Development and Process Improvement at LBI

Lawrence Brothers has demonstrated a strong commitment to workforce development and process improvement, recognizing that the skills of its workforce and the efficiency of its operations are essential to its competitive advantage. The company has worked with GENEDGE, part of the MEP National Network, on value stream mapping and manufacturing process improvement projects, as well as a lean manufacturing class conducted in November 2022 . These initiatives have resulted in significant improvements in the company's operations.

The value stream mapping exercises revealed opportunities for improvement that had not been apparent to the company's leadership, leading to the implementation of process improvements that resulted in nearly 40 percent more efficiency . These process improvements did not require capital investment, demonstrating the value of process optimization in improving productivity. The time and money saved through these improvements enabled the company to fund research and development in automation exploration and on-site apprenticeship programs. The company has achieved a 35 percent increase in efficiency on the production floor and has been able to reduce overtime from 15 percent per pay period to 7 percent, allowing net income to increase .

The company's investment in workforce development includes an on-site paid apprenticeship program that upskills both incoming workers and existing employees in higher-skilled and more competitively compensated career paths such as automation programming, maintenance, and operation . The apprenticeship program is a critical component of the company's strategy to address workforce challenges, developing the skilled labor needed to operate its advanced equipment. The program provides career pathways for workers, enabling them to advance from entry-level positions to higher-skilled roles that offer greater compensation and job satisfaction.

The collaboration between Lawrence Brothers and regional partners is essential to the company's workforce development efforts. Brandon McCracken, Lawrence Brothers' COO, noted that "through collaborative regional partnerships like those with other local manufacturers and facilitators like GENEDGE, the future is bright not only for LBI, but for broader SWVA" . This recognition of the importance of collaboration reflects the company's commitment to the region and its understanding that the success of the manufacturing sector depends on the development of a skilled workforce.

### The Role of VCEDA in Supporting Manufacturing Growth

The Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority (VCEDA) has played a critical role in supporting the growth of manufacturing in the Tazewell County region, providing financing that enables companies to invest in equipment and facilities. VCEDA was created by the Virginia General Assembly in 1988 to enhance and diversify Southwest Virginia's economy and to help create jobs . The Authority provides loans and grants to businesses, supporting expansion and modernization projects that might otherwise not be possible.

VCEDA has provided significant support to Lawrence Brothers, with the most recent loan of $1.315 million for the fiber optic laser expansion closing in April 2025 . This loan follows a previous $600,000 loan in 2019, which was paid in full ahead of schedule . The company's ability to repay the loan ahead of schedule reflects the success of its expansion and the strength of its business model. The partnership between VCEDA and Lawrence Brothers is an example of how public financing can support private sector growth, creating jobs and economic activity that benefits the broader region.

VCEDA has also supported other manufacturers in the region, including Simmons Equipment Company, which received a $187,500 workforce development and training grant, as well as a $500,000 loan to the Industrial Development Authority of Russell County for facility improvements . The Authority's support for manufacturing is part of a broader strategy of economic diversification, recognizing that manufacturing provides stable, well-paying jobs that are essential to the region's economic future.

The financing programs offered by VCEDA include revolving loan funds, which provide low-interest loans for projects that support economic and workforce development. The Coalfield Revolving Loan Fund and the Communities for Opportunity Loan Fund provide financing for fixed asset needs such as building construction, expansion, or rehabilitation, or the purchase of equipment, machinery, and tools . These programs are available to for-profit businesses, non-profits, and industrial or economic development authorities in the region, providing the financing needed for investment and growth.

### Simmons Equipment Company: Soft Rock Mining Equipment Manufacturer

Simmons Equipment Company represents another significant manufacturing success story in the Tazewell County region, with the company founded in Tazewell County in 2005 and expanding its operations into neighboring Russell County in 2024 . The company is a soft rock mining equipment manufacturer that employs cutting-edge engineering and technology to offer customized solutions to meet customer's needs, providing equipment that unleashes maximum productivity while ensuring the health and safety of the miners who use it . The company's commitment to safety and productivity has made it a leader in the soft rock mining equipment sector.

Simmons Equipment manufactures a wide range of equipment for the soft rock mining sector, including battery and diesel-powered scoops, haulers, and longwall support vehicles . The battery-powered scoops and haulers are an important component of the transition to cleaner mining technologies, reducing diesel emissions in underground mines. The longwall support vehicles provide essential support for longwall mining operations, ensuring the safety and efficiency of the mining process. The company's product range demonstrates its expertise in the engineering and fabrication of mining equipment.

The company's expansion into Russell County represents a significant investment in the region's manufacturing sector. Simmons Equipment is investing $8.5 million to convert two buildings in Lebanon, an investment that is expected to create 75 jobs . The expansion is being supported by VCEDA, the Virginia Economic Development Partnership, and the Commonwealth's Opportunity Fund, which approved a $270,000 grant to assist Russell County with the project . The expansion will also allow for further product line expansion, positioning the company for continued growth in the soft rock mining sector.

The decision to expand in Russell County was based on the area's trained workforce, high-quality facilities, and the business-friendly spirit of the region and state . The company visited a number of sites in West Virginia and Tennessee before choosing Russell County, demonstrating the competitiveness of the region for manufacturing investment. The availability of a skilled workforce, developed through generations of industrial employment, was a key factor in the company's decision to expand in the region.

### The Economic Impact of Simmons Equipment's Expansion

The expansion of Simmons Equipment into Russell County has significant economic implications for the broader region, creating jobs, generating tax revenue, and supporting the growth of the manufacturing sector. The 75 jobs that the company expects to create represent a substantial addition to the region's employment base, providing stable, well-paying jobs for workers in the area. The jobs are in manufacturing, a sector that provides wages that are typically higher than the average for the region.

The company's investment of $8.5 million in facility development and equipment will generate economic activity during the construction and installation phase, with local contractors and suppliers benefiting from the project . The long-term economic impact of the expansion includes the ongoing spending of the company and its employees, which ripples through the local economy. The company's presence in the region also makes the area more attractive to other businesses, creating a virtuous cycle of investment and growth.

The support that Simmons Equipment has received from VCEDA and other economic development organizations reflects the strategic importance of the manufacturing sector to the region's economic future. The Virginia Economic Development Partnership worked with Russell County and VCEDA to secure the project for Virginia, demonstrating the collaborative approach to economic development that characterizes the region . The company is also eligible to receive state benefits from the Major Business Facility Job Tax Credit for new, full-time jobs created .

The expansion of Simmons Equipment is part of a broader trend of manufacturing growth in the Tazewell County region, with companies investing in new facilities and equipment to serve growing markets. The region's manufacturing sector has demonstrated remarkable resilience in the face of the decline of the coal industry, adapting to new market conditions and embracing new technologies. The success of companies like Simmons Equipment and Lawrence Brothers provides a foundation for continued manufacturing growth in the region.

West River Conveyors, highlighted by the Southwest Virginia Alliance for Manufacturing, is a company that builds custom conveyor systems and heavy-duty components for industries including mining, aggregates, sand and gravel, and other material handling applications . ### Modular Housing: A New Manufacturing Sector

The development of a modular housing manufacturing facility in Russell County represents a significant new manufacturing sector for the region, with the potential to create 89 jobs within five years and to address the critical shortage of workforce housing in the region . The project, supported by a $3 million loan from VCEDA to the Russell County Industrial Development Authority, will involve the purchase and renovation of the former 92,000-square-foot Buster Brown Building in the Russell County Industrial Park . The facility aims to produce at least 260 modular units in its first year and expand to 300–400 annually as the workforce grows .

The modular housing project is being developed by Appalachian Highlands Housing Partners (AHHP), a subsidiary of the Wellspring Foundation of Southwest Virginia, which identified workforce housing as one of the greatest needs in its service area . AHHP was created to provide housing for those earning between 80% and 120% of the area median income, addressing the gap between affordable housing and market-rate housing. The project will provide high-quality, cost-effective homes for the community while also equipping local workers with in-demand skills in modern construction techniques.

The modular housing project includes a workforce development component, recognizing that laborers are needed to work in the facility and that training is required to develop the skills needed for modern construction techniques . The project will provide lasting economic and social impact, with Mayana Rice, AHHP director, noting that the initiative "not only delivers high-quality, cost-effective homes for our community but also equips local workers with in-demand skills in modern construction techniques" . The workforce development component is essential to the project's success, ensuring that the jobs created are filled by workers with the skills needed to perform them.

The homes produced at the facility will be marketed throughout the region through partnerships with housing agencies and other advertising strategies, with homes available throughout the entire VCEDA region, which includes Buchanan, Dickenson, Lee, Russell, Scott, Tazewell, and Wise counties and the city of Norton . The regional marketing strategy ensures that the benefits of the project are spread across the region, providing housing that addresses the needs of communities throughout Southwest Virginia. The project also helps to alleviate the workforce housing shortage for the region, a critical need that has constrained economic development.

# Chapter Nine: Pillar Three - Manufacturing and Machine Making

## Section 9.2: Simmons Equipment Company - Expansion Strategy and Technological Integration

### The Foundation of Simmons Equipment Company

Simmons Equipment Company stands as one of the most significant manufacturing success stories in the Tazewell County region, representing the resilience and adaptability of the industrial sector in the face of economic transformation. Founded in Tazewell County in 2005, the company has grown from a regional manufacturer into a global supplier of soft rock mining equipment, employing cutting-edge engineering and technology to offer customized solutions that meet customer needs while ensuring the health and safety of the miners who use its equipment . The company's commitment to innovation and quality has enabled it to compete successfully in international markets, expanding its customer base far beyond the Appalachian coalfields.

The company's product line reflects its expertise in the engineering and fabrication of mining equipment, with a focus on battery and diesel-powered scoops, haulers, and longwall support vehicles that are essential to modern underground mining operations . The battery-powered scoops and haulers are particularly significant, as they represent the industry's transition to cleaner mining technologies that reduce diesel emissions in underground mines. The longwall support vehicles provide essential support for longwall mining operations, ensuring the safety and efficiency of the mining process. The company's product range demonstrates its expertise in the engineering and fabrication of mining equipment and its commitment to serving the evolving needs of the mining industry.

The company's success has been built on a foundation of innovation and customer service, with a focus on developing equipment that enhances productivity while ensuring the safety of miners. The company's engineering team works closely with customers to develop customized solutions that address specific operational challenges, providing equipment that is tailored to the unique conditions of each mine. This commitment to customization has enabled the company to build strong relationships with customers, creating a loyal customer base that has supported the company's growth.

### The $8.5 Million Expansion Investment

The most significant development in Simmons Equipment's recent history is the $8.5 million expansion investment announced in July 2024, which will see the company establish manufacturing operations in neighboring Russell County while maintaining its existing facilities and employees in Tazewell County . The expansion represents a major commitment to the Southwest Virginia region, creating 75 new jobs and generating economic activity that extends well beyond the direct employment at the facility. The company considered a number of sites in West Virginia and Tennessee before choosing Russell County, demonstrating the competitiveness of the region for manufacturing investment .

The expansion into Russell County reflects the company's growth into new markets worldwide, with the additional manufacturing capacity enabling the company to aggressively pursue opportunities in international markets . The company's business has been growing internationally, and the expansion will allow it to meet increasing demand for its soft rock mining equipment. The decision to expand in Southwest Virginia, rather than relocating to another state, reflects the company's commitment to the region and its confidence in the workforce that has supported its growth.

The expansion involves converting two buildings in Lebanon, Russell County, with the company planning to invest $8.5 million in facility development and equipment . The facility development will include the renovation of existing buildings to meet the company's manufacturing needs, as well as the installation of new equipment that will enhance the company's production capabilities. The expansion will also allow for further product line expansion, as the company develops new products to serve its growing customer base . The investment represents a significant commitment to the region and a vote of confidence in the region's manufacturing workforce.

The expansion is being supported by a range of state and regional incentive programs, reflecting the strategic importance of the manufacturing sector to the region's economic future. The Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority has approved a $187,500 workforce development and training grant to the company, as well as a $500,000 loan to the Industrial Development Authority of Russell County for facility improvements . The Virginia Economic Development Partnership worked with Russell County and the Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority to secure the project for Virginia, with Governor Youngkin approving a $270,000 grant from the Commonwealth's Opportunity Fund to assist Russell County with the project . The company is also eligible to receive state benefits from the Major Business Facility Job Tax Credit for new, full-time jobs created .

### The lowL Compiler: A Tool for Advanced Manufacturing

The lowL compiler, a complete implementation of a systems programming language for Intel x86_64 architectures, represents a significant tool for the advancement of manufacturing in the region, providing the capability to develop real-time control systems that can operate the sophisticated equipment that companies like Simmons Equipment produce. The compiler, implemented in Python with version 2.1.0, provides a comprehensive set of features for systems programming, including inline assembly, hardware builtins, SIMD vector types, and optimization levels from O0 to O3 [citation:compiler]. These features enable the development of control systems that are both efficient and reliable, essential for the operation of mining equipment.

The compiler's support for inline assembly (the `asm` statement) enables developers to incorporate assembly code directly into their high-level lowL programs, providing the control needed for real-time hardware interaction. This is essential for mining equipment control systems, where precise timing and direct hardware access are required. The compiler's hardware builtins, including `cli`, `sti`, `hlt`, `pause`, `rdtsc`, and `cpuid`, provide access to the processor's capabilities, enabling developers to write code that is optimized for the specific hardware platform [citation:compiler]. The compiler's support for SIMD vector types, including `vec4_f32`, `vec8_f32`, and `vec16_f32`, enables parallel processing that can significantly accelerate computationally intensive tasks such as sensor fusion and real-time control.

The compiler's data sections feature, which includes support for external file formats such as CSV, JSON, XML, YAML, and TOML, provides a mechanism for managing configuration data and precomputed motion profiles [citation:compiler]. This is particularly useful for manufacturing equipment control systems, where configuration parameters and motion profiles need to be managed efficiently. The compiler's `#[interrupt]` and `#[kernel]` attributes provide the capability to develop interrupt handlers and kernel modules, essential for real-time control systems that must respond quickly to events [citation:compiler]. The compiler's support for optimization levels from O0 to O3 provides the flexibility to balance code size and performance, enabling developers to optimize for the specific requirements of their application.

The compiler's pattern matching switch statements, with `when` guards and priority, provide a powerful mechanism for handling complex control logic, enabling developers to write code that is both readable and efficient [citation:compiler]. The compiler's BlockArray data structure, which is SIMD-optimized, provides a high-performance dynamic array that is suitable for real-time applications. The compiler's support for memory management builtins, including `physical_alloc` and `physical_free`, provides the capability to manage memory efficiently, essential for embedded systems with limited resources [citation:compiler]. The compiler's module system supports code organization and reuse, enabling developers to build complex systems from modular components.

The lowL compiler is particularly well-suited to the development of control systems for mining equipment, where real-time performance, reliability, and efficiency are essential. The compiler's support for x86_64 architectures makes it compatible with the embedded processors used in mining equipment, while its features enable developers to write code that is optimized for the specific requirements of the application. The compiler's open-source nature enables customization and extension, ensuring that it can adapt to the evolving needs of the manufacturing industry.

### Robotics and Artificial Intelligence Integration

The integration of robotics and artificial intelligence into manufacturing is transforming the industry, enabling companies like Simmons Equipment to develop equipment that is smarter, more efficient, and safer than traditional mining equipment. The "Algorithmic Movement" book provides a comprehensive framework for the development of intelligent robotic systems, with the lowL language serving as the foundation for real-time control [citation:robotics_book]. The book's philosophy of "Algorithmic Movement" – that every motion is the direct execution of an algorithm – is directly applicable to the development of mining equipment control systems, where precise, deterministic control is essential.

The thin client model of robotics, where intelligence is centralized and movement is distributed, is particularly relevant to the development of mining equipment, where the complexity of the control system can be managed by a central processor while the actuators are controlled by simple, reliable hardware. The book's reference hardware architectures, including the ultra-low-cost design for a dancing toy and the mid-range design for a home companion, demonstrate the range of possibilities for robotics integration [citation:robotics_book]. The modular design of AlgorithmicOS, the operating system for robots written entirely in lowL, provides a framework for developing control systems that can be adapted to the specific requirements of different mining equipment.

The book's detailed specifications for products such as the Spark dancing robot and the Nova home companion demonstrate the application of the Algorithmic Movement philosophy to real-world robotics [citation:robotics_book]. The Spark firmware, written in lowL, provides a complete example of a real-time control system that receives commands via wireless communication and controls servos and motors with precision. The firmware's data sections for precomputed dance sequences demonstrate the efficient management of motion profiles, an approach that can be applied to the development of mining equipment control systems.

The book's discussion of safety systems is particularly relevant to mining equipment, where safety is paramount. The emergency stop handler, implemented as a high-priority interrupt, provides a model for safety-critical systems that must respond quickly to emergency conditions [citation:robotics_book]. The safety monitor, which checks for watchdog timeouts, battery voltage, motor current, and physical stalls, demonstrates the comprehensive approach to safety that is essential for mining equipment. The integration of multiple sensors, including LIDAR, depth cameras, and force-torque sensors, enables the development of equipment that can perceive its environment and respond appropriately.

The application of the Algorithmic Movement framework to manufacturing equipment would enable the development of systems that are smarter, more efficient, and safer than traditional equipment. The integration of AI, through the use of centralized intelligence, would enable equipment to learn from experience, to adapt to changing conditions, and to optimize its performance. The use of lowL as the foundation for control systems would ensure that the equipment is reliable, efficient, and maintainable. The Algorithmic Movement framework thus provides a pathway for the development of next-generation manufacturing equipment.

### Advanced Robotics in Manufacturing Applications

The integration of advanced robotics into manufacturing applications is transforming the industry, with robotic systems capable of handling a wide range of tasks from material handling to precision machining. The Simmons Machine Tool Corporation case study, which describes the use of automated material handling systems for railway wheel maintenance, provides a compelling example of how robotics can enhance productivity and safety in heavy manufacturing . The system, which uses a ZP-6 overhead gantry with a two-axis robot from Güdel, automates the process of moving heavy railway wheels into and out of machining centers, eliminating the need for manual handling with forklifts or cranes .

The automated wheel maintenance system addresses two key challenges in railway maintenance: worker safety and productivity. The traditional process of moving railway wheels, which can weigh up to 900 pounds, using forklifts or cranes was slow and inconsistent, and it posed significant safety risks to workers . The automated system, which is integrated with a vertical CNC lathe, enables consistent, reliable handling of the wheels while protecting workers from the dangers of moving heavy objects in tight spaces . The system also includes measurement capabilities that enable precise boring of the wheel center holes, ensuring a precise interference fit with the axle .

The success of the Simmons Machine Tool system demonstrates the potential for robotics to transform heavy manufacturing, with automated systems capable of handling materials that would be difficult or dangerous for human workers to manage. The system is part of a broader trend toward automation in manufacturing, with robotic systems increasingly capable of handling tasks that were previously performed manually. The integration of robotics with CNC machining centers enables fully automated cells that can operate continuously, with minimal human intervention.

The robotics applications in manufacturing extend beyond material handling to include welding, assembly, inspection, and packaging. The petfood co-packer case study, which describes the use of a robotic case-packing system to load split-cup petfood packs into custom shelf-ready trays and cases, demonstrates the potential for robotics in packaging applications . The system, which uses Cama North America's IF318 monobloc loading system with Y-shaped, 2-axis native Cama robots, is engineered to orient cups, form cases from blanks, insert plastic trays into cases, load cups into trays, and close the cases, all in one compact frame . The system handles 600 to 700 cups per minute, demonstrating the high productivity that robotics can achieve .

The development of robotic systems for manufacturing requires a comprehensive approach to engineering, combining mechanical design, electrical engineering, software development, and systems integration. The mechanical design must account for the forces, speeds, and accuracies required for the application, while the electrical engineering must provide the power and control systems needed to operate the equipment. The software development, which may be done in languages like lowL, must provide the control algorithms, safety systems, and user interfaces that enable the equipment to operate effectively. The systems integration ensures that all components work together seamlessly, providing a reliable and productive manufacturing system.

The integration of advanced robotics and AI into Simmons Equipment's manufacturing operations represents a significant opportunity to enhance productivity, quality, and safety, building on the company's existing expertise in the design and fabrication of mining equipment. The company's existing product lines, including battery and diesel-powered scoops, haulers, and longwall support vehicles, could be enhanced with the integration of robotic systems that automate tasks such as welding, assembly, and inspection . The integration of AI into Simmons Equipment's products would enable the development of smart mining equipment that can monitor its own performance, detect potential problems, and adjust its operation to optimize efficiency and safety. The use of AI for predictive maintenance, for example, would enable the equipment to detect signs of wear or impending failure before they cause a breakdown, reducing downtime and maintenance costs. The use of AI for performance optimization would enable the equipment to adjust its operation to maximize productivity while minimizing energy consumption, reducing operating costs for customers.

The application of the lowL compiler and AlgorithmicOS to Simmons Equipment's products would provide a robust, reliable, and efficient software platform for the development of control systems. The compiler's support for real-time operation, with deterministic performance and low latency, would ensure that the equipment responds quickly and predictably to operator commands and sensor inputs. The compiler's support for SIMD operations would accelerate computationally intensive tasks such as sensor fusion and real-time control, enhancing the performance of the equipment. The compiler's support for hardware builtins would provide direct access to the processor's capabilities, enabling developers to write code that is optimized for the specific hardware platform.

The workforce development component of the Simmons Equipment expansion provides an opportunity to develop the skills needed for the integration of robotics and AI into the company's operations. The training program, supported by the VCEDA workforce development and training grant, can be expanded to include training in robotics, AI, and systems integration, preparing workers for the jobs of the future . The partnership between Simmons Equipment, VCEDA, and local training providers can create a pipeline of skilled workers who can develop, operate, and maintain the advanced manufacturing systems that will define the future of the company.

# Chapter Nine: Pillar Three - Manufacturing and Machine Making

## Section 9.2: Diversified Manufacturing - A Comprehensive Strategy for Growth

### The Strategic Imperative for Manufacturing Diversification

The diversification of Tazewell County's manufacturing base represents one of the most critical components of the region's economic development strategy, building on the industrial heritage established during the coal era while embracing new industries, new technologies, and new markets. The manufacturing sector in the broader Southwest Virginia region has demonstrated remarkable resilience and adaptability, transitioning from a dependence on the coal industry to a diversified base that serves data centers, infrastructure, energy, construction, and materials handling. The success of companies like Tate, VFP, West River Conveyors, and White Rock Truss demonstrates the potential for manufacturing to thrive in the region, even as the coal industry continues its long-term decline.

The strategic importance of manufacturing diversification to the region's economy cannot be overstated. The sector provides stable, well-paying jobs that support families and communities, generating economic activity that ripples through the broader economy. The manufacturing sector also provides a market for the region's workforce, utilizing the skills that have been developed over generations of industrial employment. The presence of a strong, diversified manufacturing base makes the region more attractive to other businesses, creating a virtuous cycle of investment and growth that supports the broader economic development of the region.

The manufacturing sector in the Southwest Virginia region is characterized by its diversity, with companies serving a range of industries including data center infrastructure, utility protection, materials handling, and construction products. This diversity provides a buffer against downturns in any single industry, as companies can shift their production to serve other markets when demand in one sector declines. The diversity of the manufacturing sector is a strength that has enabled the region to weather the decline of coal and to build a more resilient industrial base for the future.

The support of the Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority (VCEDA) and other economic development organizations has been essential to the growth and diversification of the manufacturing sector. VCEDA has provided loans and grants to manufacturing companies in the region, supporting expansion and modernization projects that have created jobs and increased competitiveness. The partnership between the public and private sectors has been essential to the success of the manufacturing sector, with the public sector providing the financing and infrastructure that enable private companies to invest and grow. The VFP expansion in Scott County, the White Rock Truss expansion in Lee County, and the Tate expansion in Russell County all demonstrate the effectiveness of this partnership model.

---

### Tate: Data Center Components Manufacturing in Russell County

#### The Expansion and Its Significance

Tate, a global leader in mission-critical data center infrastructure solutions and a Kingspan company, has significantly expanded its U.S. manufacturing footprint with the acquisition of a 131,000-square-foot facility in Lebanon, Russell County, Virginia . The company will occupy the former Alcoa manufacturing building at 705 Regional Park Road, which has been acquired and renovated through a collaborative effort led by the Russell County Industrial Development Authority (IDA) and the Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority (VCEDA) . The expansion was announced in December 2025 and represents a significant commitment to the region's manufacturing sector.

The former Alcoa building, originally constructed in the late 1990s, contains more than 130,000 square feet of manufacturing and office space, situated on an approximately 11-acre site adjacent to four-lane U.S. Route 19 . The facility features 13 docks and/or overhead doors, providing excellent logistical capabilities for manufacturing operations . The building was previously used as an automotive wheel manufacturing plant until it closed more than a decade ago, and its reactivation represents a significant economic development success for Russell County . The facility is located just 18 miles from the company's existing St. Paul site, enabling operational synergies and shared resources .

The expansion is expected to create up to 35 new full-time jobs within three years, providing employment opportunities for workers in the region . The jobs will include positions in manufacturing, quality control, logistics, and management, offering career pathways for workers with diverse skills and experience. The economic impact of the expansion extends beyond the direct jobs created, encompassing the broader economic benefits of increased economic activity, tax revenue, and supply chain development .

The expansion was supported by an up to $4 million loan from VCEDA to the Russell County IDA, which enabled the IDA to purchase and prepare the building for new industrial use . The loan facilitated the acquisition of the building, the performance of basic renovations, and associated costs, preparing the facility for Tate's manufacturing operations . The Russell County IDA and VCEDA collaborated on the project, with the IDA acquiring the building and Tate leasing the upgraded facility .

#### Tate's Role in the Data Center Infrastructure Market

Tate is a global leader in advanced interior infrastructure solutions for mission-critical data centers, with more than 120 years of industry heritage . The company has evolved from a raised access floor manufacturer to a comprehensive provider of advanced data center systems, including modular aisle containment, raised access floors, structural ceilings, airflow management solutions, and liquid-cooling technologies . Tate's portfolio delivers floor-to-ceiling performance, enabling customers to build efficient, scalable, and sustainable data centers .

The company's products are essential to the operation of modern data centers, which form the backbone of the digital economy. The modular aisle containment systems provide thermal management that improves energy efficiency, while the raised access floors enable flexible cabling and airflow management . The structural ceilings and airflow management solutions optimize the data center environment, and the liquid-cooling technologies enable high-density computing applications . The company's products are used by hyperscale, colocation, and enterprise customers, supporting the nation's digital infrastructure .

The demand for data center infrastructure has grown substantially in recent years, driven by the expansion of cloud computing, artificial intelligence, and digital services . The proliferation of data centers across the United States, particularly in Virginia's data center corridor, has created significant demand for the infrastructure components that Tate produces . The company's expansion in Russell County positions it to meet this growing demand, strengthening its North American production footprint and manufacturing resiliency .

The company's commitment to the region is reflected in the words of Daniel Kennedy, global president of Cloud Solutions & Innovation with Tate: "Growing our presence in Russell County allows us to create new jobs and contribute to the region's long-term economic vitality. We're proud to continue investing in a community that plays a key role in supporting the nation's digital infrastructure" . This commitment demonstrates Tate's confidence in the region's workforce and its potential for continued economic growth.

#### The Role of VCEDA and the Russell County IDA

The partnership between VCEDA, the Russell County IDA, and Tate exemplifies the collaborative approach to economic development that characterizes the region. VCEDA approved an up to $4 million loan enabling the IDA to purchase and prepare the building for new industrial use . The loan was used to acquire the building, perform basic renovations, and associated costs, ensuring that the facility met the requirements for modern manufacturing operations . The Russell County IDA, led by Executive Director Ernie McFaddin, demonstrated vision in acquiring the building and developing a plan to return it to productive use .

The Russell County IDA's role in the project was essential, as it provided the public sector capacity to acquire and prepare the facility for private sector use . The IDA's ability to take on this responsibility reflects the strength of the region's economic development infrastructure and the commitment of its leadership to fostering economic growth . The IDA's Chairman Ron Blankenship noted, "We are glad that Tate chose Russell County to expand their manufacturing operations and that the Russell County IDA was able to assist them in securing this property" .

VCEDA Executive Director/General Counsel Jonathan Belcher praised the collaborative effort, stating: "VCEDA is pleased to see Tate expanding its operations to the former Alcoa building. Tate has been a great employer in the region and we are happy to see them continue to grow. VCEDA would also like to commend Ernie McFaddin and the Russell County Industrial Development Authority for the vision to acquire and put in place a plan to get the former Alcoa building once again in productive use and creating new jobs" . This statement reflects VCEDA's commitment to supporting manufacturing growth and its appreciation for the collaborative efforts that make such projects possible.

The success of the Tate expansion demonstrates the effectiveness of the public-private partnership model that has been developed in the region. The public sector provides the financing and infrastructure that enable private companies to invest and grow, while the private sector provides the entrepreneurship, innovation, and market knowledge that drive economic development . The partnership model has been successful in attracting and retaining manufacturing companies in the region, creating jobs and economic activity that benefit the broader community.

#### The Future of the Tate Facility

The former Alcoa building, which will reopen on December 1 to support Tate's manufacturing operations , has been renovated to meet the company's modern manufacturing requirements . The 131,000-square-foot facility provides significant production capacity, enabling the company to expand its manufacturing operations and meet growing customer demand . The facility's location adjacent to U.S. Route 19 provides excellent transportation access, and its 13 docks and overhead doors support efficient logistics operations .

The Tate expansion is expected to create up to 35 new full-time jobs within three years, providing employment opportunities for workers in Russell County and the surrounding region . The jobs will include positions in manufacturing, quality control, logistics, and management, offering career pathways for workers with diverse skills and experience . The creation of these jobs will support families and communities, generating economic activity that ripples through the broader economy .

The Tate expansion also contributes to the broader economic development strategy for the region, supporting the growth of the data center infrastructure industry in Southwest Virginia . The region has become an important location for data center infrastructure manufacturing, with companies like Tate and VFP serving the growing demand for data center components . The expansion of this industry cluster creates opportunities for supply chain development, workforce training, and continued economic growth .

The success of the Tate facility may also encourage additional manufacturing investment in the region, as other companies see the potential for success in the area . The availability of a skilled workforce, the support of regional economic development organizations, and the strategic location of the region all contribute to its attractiveness for manufacturing investment . The Tate expansion thus serves as a catalyst for further economic development, demonstrating the potential for manufacturing success in the region.

### VFP, Inc.: Expanding in Scott County with a $35 Million Investment

#### The Expansion and Its Significance

VFP, Inc., a producer of custom-designed enclosures used to protect critical infrastructure, has announced a significant $35 million expansion of its operations in Scott County, representing one of the largest manufacturing investments in the region's recent history . The expansion, which was announced by Governor Glenn Youngkin in October 2025, is expected to create 200 new jobs at VFP's Duffield manufacturing campus . The project coincides with the company's 60th anniversary and represents its third and largest plant expansion in the last five years .

The expansion will double the company's production capacity, enabling it to meet surging demand for its products in the Commonwealth and beyond . The company's growth has been driven by the expanding data center and utility power industries, which require the custom enclosures that VFP manufactures . The expansion positions VFP to support continued growth within these industries, strengthening the company's market position and creating new employment opportunities in the region .

VFP was founded in 1965 and is headquartered in Roanoke, Virginia . The company manufactures custom building solutions designed to protect critical equipment, with a range of products from heavyweight concrete shelters to lightweight, flexible metal shelters that can be customized to meet diverse client needs . The company's products are used predominantly by utility providers, municipalities, and broadband providers and can be found in all 50 states, 82 countries, and on all seven continents .

The company's history in Southwest Virginia dates back nearly 30 years, when VFP relocated manufacturing to Scott County in 1997 . This decision has proven instrumental to the company's long-term success, positioning it geographically to support Virginia's robust data center business . The company's commitment to the region is reflected in its continued investment in its Duffield facility and its engagement with the local community .

#### Economic Impact and Job Creation

The economic impact of the VFP expansion is substantial, with the $35 million investment representing a significant commitment to the region's manufacturing sector . The investment includes the expansion of manufacturing facilities, the purchase of new equipment, and the development of workforce training programs . The expansion is expected to double the company's production capacity, enabling it to meet growing demand for its products and to serve new markets .

The creation of 200 new jobs represents a significant addition to the region's employment base, providing stable, well-paying jobs for workers in Scott County and the surrounding region . The jobs will include positions in manufacturing, quality control, engineering, and management, offering career pathways for workers with diverse skills and experience . The company's employee-ownership model, which gives workers a stake in the company's success, makes VFP an attractive employer in the region .

The expansion builds on VFP's previous investments in Scott County, including a $5 million expansion creating 50 jobs in 2024 and a $7.2 million expansion creating 30 jobs in 2021 . In 2024, the Duffield facility employed 350 workers, and with this latest expansion, total employment is expected to exceed 500 workers . This consistent growth demonstrates the company's commitment to the region and its success in the manufacturing sector.

The economic impact of the VFP expansion extends beyond the direct jobs created, encompassing the broader economic benefits of increased economic activity, tax revenue, and supply chain development . The expansion will generate demand for construction services, materials, and equipment, creating economic activity during the development phase . The long-term economic impact includes the ongoing spending of the company and its employees, which ripples through the local economy, supporting businesses and services in the region .

#### VCEDA Support and Public-Private Partnership

The VFP expansion is being supported by a range of state and regional incentive programs, reflecting the strategic importance of the manufacturing sector to the region's economic future . The Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority has approved a $312,500 grant to VFP to support the company's workforce development and training, as well as a $3 million loan earmarked for the Scott County Economic Development Authority to support equipment acquisition as the expansion is developed .

The Commonwealth's support for the project is significant, with Governor Youngkin approving an $800,000 grant from the Commonwealth's Opportunity Fund to assist Scott County with the project, as well as a $350,000 Virginia Investment Performance Grant . Support for VFP's job creation will also be provided through the Virginia Talent Accelerator Program, which offers recruitment and training services at no cost to eligible companies expanding or locating assets in Virginia .

An innovative first-of-its-kind $1 million award through the new Workforce Housing Economic Development Incentive Pilot Program will create new housing opportunities for VFP's rapidly growing workforce . The funds will be used to support a major new housing development in the county, leverage significant additional funding to support it, and feature at least 50 new housing units reserved for households earning between 80 to 150 percent of the Area Median Income . This innovative approach addresses the housing challenges that can constrain economic growth, ensuring that the new jobs created by the expansion can be filled by workers who have access to affordable housing.

The partnership between VFP, VCEDA, Scott County, the Virginia Tobacco Region Revitalization Commission, and the Virginia Economic Development Partnership demonstrates the collaborative approach to economic development that characterizes the region . Delegate Terry Kilgore, a member of the Tobacco Commission, noted: "I am thrilled with VFP's growth and expansion in Scott County and pleased that the Tobacco Commission could support this project in an innovative and meaningful way. This new workforce housing program is a perfect example of the Commission's commitment to listening to our communities and working creatively to meet the needs of their residents and employers" .

#### The Company's Long-Term Commitment to the Region

VFP's commitment to the Southwest Virginia region is reflected in its consistent investment in the Duffield facility and its engagement with the local community. President and CEO Scott File noted: "VFP's decision to relocate manufacturing to Scott County nearly 30 years ago has proven instrumental to our long-term success, and positions VFP geographically in a prime location to support Virginia's robust data center business. We recognize our success is directly tied to the dedication and talents of our employee-owners and are appreciative of the continued support and assistance received from local, regional, and state agencies" .

The company's employee-ownership model is a distinctive feature that contributes to its success and its appeal as an employer. Employees who have a stake in the company's success are more engaged and committed, contributing to the quality and productivity of the operation . The model also provides workers with a share in the company's success, creating a sense of ownership and pride in the work they do .

The VFP expansion contributes to the broader economic development of Scott County and the surrounding region, creating jobs, generating tax revenue, and building a stronger manufacturing base . The company's products are essential to the critical infrastructure that supports the modern economy, and its success positions the region as a center for manufacturing excellence . The expansion also demonstrates the effectiveness of the public-private partnership model that has been developed in the region, providing a template for future economic development projects .

### West River Conveyors: Expanding in Buchanan County with VCEDA Support

#### The Relocation and Expansion

West River Conveyors and Machinery Company, one of the largest manufacturers and private employers in Buchanan County, is embarking on a significant relocation and expansion project with the support of an up to $4 million loan from the Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority . The loan, which closed in August 2025, will be used to finance site development, the construction of an approximately 65,000-square-foot manufacturing facility at the Southern Gap Industrial Park, as well as for the installation of utilities, concrete, paving and parking lot construction and the purchase of equipment, machinery and tools .

The relocation to Southern Gap represents a new chapter for the company, which was founded in 1981 by J. B. Roulett and Joe G. Street and incorporated in Virginia in 1985 . The company's history in Buchanan County is long and distinguished, with West River serving as a major employer and economic contributor to the community . Originally located adjacent to U.S. 460 in the Oakwood area, the company relocated some 25 years ago to a larger piece of flat property on Dismal River Road .

The need for relocation was driven by two major disasters that struck the company's previous location. In 2022, massive flooding occurred in the area of Buchanan County, and West River's facility was partially flooded and its access bridge was damaged . In December 2024, the company experienced a major fire that severely damaged its primary manufacturing space and several items of its manufacturing equipment . Although the company was able to get back into operation quickly by re-tasking some smaller buildings on the property which were not damaged by the fire, the decision was made that it was best for the long-term future of the company and its ability to grow and expand to find a new site at which to relocate .

The new site at Southern Gap is within 1,000 feet of the Route 744 intersection of the new Coalfields Expressway (U.S. 121/460), making it an excellent logistical location from which to operate and manufacture products . The strategic location provides excellent transportation access, enabling the company to serve its customers throughout the United States and internationally . The facility will be located in a modern industrial park, providing the infrastructure and services needed for efficient manufacturing operations .

#### The Company's Products and Market Position

West River Conveyors is a designer and manufacturer of conveyor systems and equipment . The company builds belt drives, discharge/transfer stations, tail sections, take-up units, power packs, starters, belt storage units, winches, structure and many unique fabrications to customer specifications . It also sells conveyor belt as part of its conveyor packages as well as new and used conveyor parts . According to its application for VCEDA funding, West River has the largest selection of new, used and surplus unused industrial gearboxes in the United States and has delivered parts and products to almost all 50 states and many foreign countries .

The company serves a variety of mining industries including coal, aggregate, gypsum, phosphate, salt, potash and trona . Additional industries served by West River with conveyor parts include paper/wood, zinc, recycling/waste management, agriculture, construction, mills, marine terminals, energy/power plants and steel . This diversification of industries served has enabled the company to weather the decline of the coal industry in the region, finding new markets for its products and services .

The company's product line includes both standard and custom conveyor equipment, providing solutions for a wide range of material handling applications . The belt drives provide the power to move materials, while the discharge/transfer stations enable the efficient transfer of materials between conveyors . The tail sections, take-up units, and belt storage units provide the structure and tensioning needed for conveyor operation . The company's custom fabrications enable it to meet unique customer requirements, providing solutions that are tailored to specific applications .

The company's market position is supported by its reputation for quality, reliability, and service . The company has a long history of serving customers in the mining industry, and its relationships with these customers have provided a foundation for expansion into other industries . The company's ability to provide both new and used equipment, as well as replacement parts, has made it a preferred supplier for companies seeking to maintain their conveyor systems .

#### VCEDA Support and Future Job Creation

The $4 million loan from VCEDA is a critical component of the West River expansion, providing the financing needed for site development, facility construction, and equipment acquisition . The loan is being provided to Apex Leasing Corporation, an affiliate of West River Conveyors and Machinery Company, which will be responsible for the construction and development of the new facility . The loan's approval reflects VCEDA's confidence in the company's business plan and its potential for continued growth .

VCEDA Executive Director/General Counsel Jonathan Belcher noted: "West River is one of VCEDA's longest-standing customers going all the way back to 1989. West River — including its affiliate Apex Leasing Corporation — has received several loans from VCEDA through the years to assist with various expansion projects. All of these loans have always been paid promptly" . This statement reflects the strength of the relationship between VCEDA and West River and the company's track record of financial responsibility .

With the expansion and new location, West River has projected it will create an additional 10 new full-time jobs within five years, an additional 10 new full-time jobs within 10 years and five more within 15 years for a total of 25 new full-time jobs . The job creation projection reflects the company's growth plans and its confidence in its ability to expand its market share . The jobs will include positions in manufacturing, engineering, quality control, logistics, and management, providing career opportunities for workers in Buchanan County .

West River President Pete Savage expressed enthusiasm for the project, stating: "We are thrilled about the opportunities this move to the Southern Gap facility brings for our employees, our customers and the community. This investment positions us to continue growing while providing world-class products and services from right here in Buchanan County" . Chief Strategic Officer Jessica Savage added: "We are so grateful to secure the loan from VCEDA and for the help we've had from the Buchanan County IDA in locating a site at Southern Gap. This loan will play a critical role in making the new facility build possible for West River. It allows us to invest more in our people, equipment and long-term growth plan. This project isn't just about expanding our capacity, but it is about building the future of our company and strengthening the manufacturing economy in Buchanan County and the entire VCEDA region" .

The relocation to Southern Gap will also benefit from the development of the Coalfields Expressway, which will provide improved transportation access to the region . The new facility's proximity to the expressway will enable West River to serve its customers more efficiently, reducing transportation costs and improving logistics . The improved transportation infrastructure will also make the region more attractive to other businesses, supporting the broader economic development of the region .

### White Rock Truss and Components: Expansion in Lee County

#### The Expansion and Its Significance

White Rock Truss & Components, LLC, a Lee County-based manufacturer of construction products, has embarked on a significant expansion project that will see the company invest $1.5 million and create 27 new jobs in the Rose Hill area . The expansion, which was celebrated with a ribbon-cutting ceremony attended by Governor Glenn Youngkin in September 2025, demonstrates the company's confidence in the Lee County workforce and its commitment to the region . The company was established in Lee County in 2005 and has grown steadily over the past two decades .

The expansion will include the construction of a new lumber shed and the upgrade of existing equipment, accommodating increasing product demand . The investment of $1.5 million represents a significant commitment to the company's growth and to the Lee County community . The company manufactures construction products including trusses and engineered lumber, which it supplies to home construction companies and home improvement retailers . The products manufactured at White Rock Truss are shipped across the Southeast and to Mexico, demonstrating the company's reach and the quality of its products .

The expansion project has been supported by a range of state and regional incentive programs, reflecting the strategic importance of the manufacturing sector to the Lee County economy . The Virginia Coalfield Economic Development Authority approved a $500,000 loan to the Lee County Economic Development Authority to assist with the purchase of equipment for the expansion project . The Commonwealth's support includes a $100,000 grant from the Commonwealth's Opportunity Fund, approved by Governor Youngkin, and a $50,000 grant from the Tobacco Region Opportunity Fund, administered by the Tobacco Region Revitalization Commission .

#### Economic Impact and Community Benefits

The economic impact of the White Rock Truss expansion is significant for Lee County, creating 27 new jobs and generating economic activity that supports the broader community . The jobs created will be in manufacturing, providing stable employment for workers in Lee County . The expansion also supports the housing and construction market with the products the company provides, contributing to the broader economic development of the region .

The company's products are used in home construction and improvement, supporting the housing market in the region and beyond . The trusses and engineered lumber manufactured at White Rock Truss can be found in Taco Bell locations in Kentucky, houses in Ohio and locally, and even in Mexico . This demonstrates the quality of the products manufactured in Lee County and their value to customers throughout the United States and internationally .

The expansion project is a continuation of a vision by the company's late founder with the company and his family to continue to grow local manufacturing and to provide quality jobs . The company's commitment to the region is reflected in its investment in the expansion and its engagement with the community . The company's general manager, Mike Kidwell, who also serves on the Lee County Board of Supervisors, noted White Rock's gratitude for the support provided for the expansion project and what the expansion will mean in the community .

Governor Youngkin, speaking at the ribbon-cutting ceremony, credited the White Rock Truss workforce for the work it does to build trusses, noting that "if it wasn't for your efforts, your leaders here would not have the confidence to expand and grow" . He highlighted the ripple effect of business growth and credited the collaboration which occurred between all parties resulting in the expansion announcement . He acknowledged VCEDA's role in funding for the project, adding, "nothing happens in Southwest Virginia without the huge work of VCEDA" .

#### VCEDA Support and Public-Private Partnership

VCEDA's support for the White Rock Truss expansion includes a $500,000 loan to the Lee County Economic Development Authority to assist with the purchase of equipment for the expansion project . VCEDA Executive Director/General Counsel Jonathan Belcher noted: "VCEDA congratulates White Rock Truss and Components and Lee County on this exciting expansion project which will not only add new jobs but will also help support the housing and construction market with the products which the company provides. VCEDA was pleased to approve a $500,000 loan to the Lee County Economic Development Authority to assist with the purchase of equipment for the expansion project. It creates good jobs and good investment in the community" .

The Virginia Economic Development Partnership (VEDP) supported White Rock Truss & Components' job creation through the Virginia Jobs Investment Program, which provides consultative services and funding to companies creating new jobs in order to support employee recruitment and training activities . This support ensures that the company has the workforce needed to staff the new positions created by the expansion .

The project funding also includes a $100,000 grant from the Commonwealth's Opportunity Fund, approved by Governor Youngkin, and a $50,000 grant from the Tobacco Region Opportunity Fund, administered by the Tobacco Region Revitalization Commission . Secretary of Agriculture and Forestry Matthew Lohr noted that one of the Commonwealth's priorities was to grow the wood products industry, adding the White Rock Truss project does just that .

Delegate Terry Kilgore, who was present for the ribbon-cutting, thanked White Rock Truss for its confidence in the Commonwealth of Virginia and Lee County, noting: "It's good to see what we make and manufacture here in Lee County will go all across the Southeast and to Mexico. You are not only succeeding here, but growing here" . Senator Todd Pillion, who grew up in Lee County, expressed his gratitude to White Rock Truss for its confidence in the county, noting: "This is proof of what happens when we all work together. Families will be able to stay here and the community looks to the future with hope and pride. It represents that Southwest Virginia is open for business and that the best days are yet to come" .
